- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-16 with SEC-TLS-03: `authTokenCmd` names a conf `token-command` and is never run as a command line, and `init#InstanceDocker` sends a registry login only over https, a unix socket or loopback http. Then (the owner's call: no production instance yet, so plain-http hosts are broken now) `DockerHost::of` (`crates/moqui-git/src/ci/driver/docker.rs`) refuses `hostProtocol` `http` (or empty) to a `hostAddress` that is not loopback (`moqui_lib::net::loopback_host`: `localhost`, 127.0.0.0/8, `::1`, `::ffff:127.x`) before anything is sent, for CI runs and for the new Rust service `org.moqui.dev.InstanceServices.call#DockerApi` (`call_docker_api`, `allow-remote="false"`, `INSTANCE_ADMIN` unless authorization is disabled, refused in sim mode, fence `RemoteCall`), through which `init#`, `start#`, `stop#`, `remove#InstanceDocker`, `get#DockerStatus` and `get#DockerLog` now make every Docker call (unix socket, https with the row's `tlsCaCert`/`tlsClientCert`/`tlsClientKey`, or loopback http); the registry-only check in `init#InstanceDocker` is gone, now general. Tests: `plain_http_to_another_host_is_refused_before_anything_is_sent`, `plain_http_to_this_machine_is_accepted_in_its_mapped_form_too`, `the_docker_api_call_answers_the_host_and_needs_instance_admin` (docker_tests.rs), `no_docker_call_goes_over_plain_http_to_another_host` and `docker_services_call_the_host_admin_api_and_keep_the_container_id` (moqui-git `service/script_ports_tests.rs`), `loopback_hosts_are_this_machine_only` (moqui-lib `net.rs`).
- **Where:** runtime/components/base/framework/service/org/moqui/dev/InstanceServices.xml:447
- **Reached by:** whoever can write an `InstanceImage` or instance host row and call `init#InstanceDocker`
- **What happens:** The field's text is split on whitespace and run as the server user with the server's environment; only the fence effect `Process` stands in front. The same services call `hostProtocol || "http"` on port 2375 and send `X-Registry-Auth` there, so the registry token and full control of that Docker host cross the network in cleartext.
- **Evidence:** `authTokenPass = ec.resource.runTokenCommand(instanceImage.authTokenCmd)`; `restClient.uri().protocol(instanceHost.hostProtocol || "http").host(String(instanceHost.hostAddress)).port(instanceHost.adminPort ? parseInt(instanceHost.adminPort, 10) : 2375)`
- **Not confirmed:** who is authorized for these services and for writes to `InstanceImage`; whether the fence refuses `Process` outside a sandboxed context only.
- **Suggested fix:** Take the token command from the conf, not a row, and require `https` or `unix` for a host that is not loopback.

#### SEC-OUT-81. An ACME challenge, name or certificate row written through the generic entity tools is served and written to disk as if an order had made it

Medium; likely (lead).

- **Where:** `crates/moqui-host/src/acme/store.rs:265-289`, `:408-438`, `:452-479`; `crates/moqui-host/src/acme/mod.rs:257-273`; `crates/moqui-host/src/acme/order.rs:194`, `:202`; `crates/moqui-entity/src/entity/sensitive.rs:46-119`. Report: round 3 `A3.md`.
- **Reached by:** a user who may write an entity generically: an `ADMIN` through Tools › Data, the entity REST of `/moqui`, or a data load. Not anonymous, not an ordinary user. The checks on these rows are in `setup#AcmeName`, which a generic write does not pass through.
- **What happens:** `moqui.server.AcmeName`, `AcmeChallenge`, `AcmeCertificate` and `AcmeOrderLock` are in none of the lists of `sensitive.rs`, and three things follow from rows written that way. First, a row `AcmeChallenge {hostName: <a name this node serves>, keyAuthDigest: <64 hex>, expireDate: <future>}` is read into memory within a second by the next `acme-tls/1` handshake, and the listener answers that handshake with a certificate carrying the digest: `read_challenges` takes every row whose `expireDate` is in the future and asks for no `AcmeName` row and no live order lock. The writer starts an order for that name at a public authority with an account key of their own, plants the digest of its key authorization, and receives a publicly trusted certificate for the site's name with a private key only they hold; the node's own key never leaves it, and nothing on System › Certificates shows the order. Second, `sync_files` runs on every member each minute and writes `runtime/cert/<hostName>/fullchain.pem` and `privkey.pem` from each `AcmeCertificate` row whose name has an `AcmeName` row, with `hostName` as a path component as stored (`valid_host`, `services.rs:83-97`, is called only in `setup_acme_name`): a row pair named `default`, `localhost` or `_wildcard.example.com` puts a chosen certificate where the listener looks for those names, and one named `../../x` makes that directory outside `runtime/cert` and writes the files in it. Third, the renewal job uses `AcmeName.directoryUrl` as stored, without the `https://` test of `setup#AcmeName` (`services.rs:283`).
- **Evidence:** `store.rs:268-276` `facade.find(CHALLENGE)?.condition(moqui_core::entity::Condition::greater("expireDate", moqui_core::user::stamp_value(now))).list()` and `:282` `challenges.insert(host, Arc::new(Challenge::new(digest, expires)));`
- **Evidence:** `mod.rs:263-272` `if acme.claim_read() { ... store::read_challenges(&factory, &reader) ... } acme.config(&name)`
- **Evidence:** `store.rs:476` `if let Err(err) = write_files(&dir, &host, &text(row, "certificatePem"), &text(row, "privateKeyPem")) {`; `:415-417` `let sub = dir.join(host);` ... `std::fs::create_dir_all(&sub).map_err(fail)?;`
- **Evidence:** `store.rs:61` `let _authz = ExecutionContext::with_current(ExecutionContext::disable_authz);`
- **Evidence:** `sensitive.rs:64` `const SECRET_CONFIG_ENTITY_KEYS: &[&str] = &["emailserver", "systemmessageremote"];` and `:108-119` `SERVICE_WRITTEN_ENTITY_KEYS` ends with `"scrapetarget", "syntheticcheck",` (no `acme` key in any list); `:168-170` `pub fn is_generic_write_refused(entity_name: &str) -> bool { is_restricted_generic_entity(entity_name) || is_service_written_entity(entity_name) }`
- **Not confirmed:** not run. A generic write was not traced to its end (`grid.rs:947`, `query_rest.rs:232` and the loader at `load_feed/loader.rs:487` were read only at their `is_generic_write_refused` call), nor whether a generic write may set an `encrypt="true"` field: the second part needs it for the key, the first does not. That `runtime/cert/default/fullchain.pem` is served as the default pair (`tls.rs`) was not read, nor whether the edge node's passthrough check changes the first part. Looked for and not found: an `AcmeName` or order-lock test in `read_challenges` and `challenge_config`; a cap on `expireDate`; a `valid_host` call in `store.rs`; the ACME entities in the restricted and service-written lists; an `authorize-skip` or other limit on the entity definitions (`ServerEntities.xml:1016-1072`). What holds: `foreign_dir` refuses a directory that exists with no marker (`store.rs:409-414`), so an existing directory is not written over or later removed; `privateKeyPem` is `encrypt="true"`, which no generic answer holds (`sensitive.rs:17-18`), so the node's own key is not read this way.
- **Suggested fix:** Add the four ACME entities to `SERVICE_WRITTEN_ENTITY_KEYS`. In `read_challenges` take a row only when its name has an `AcmeName` row and its `expireDate` is within `CHALLENGE_OPEN` of now. Call `valid_host` on each `hostName` in `sync_files`, `write_files` and `remove_files`, and test `directoryUrl` in `order::issue`.
- **Re-check:** Read `entity/sensitive.rs:46-119` (no `acme` key in any restricted list) and `acme/store.rs:262-289` (every unexpired `AcmeChallenge` row is loaded). Who may write: a holder of the generic entity tools (Tools › Data, entity REST). Not read by the lead: `store.rs:452-479` (`hostName` as a path), and whether a generic write reaches these entities' authorization.

#### SEC-OUT-82. A mail OAuth provider row's `tokenUrl` receives the client secret and users' refresh tokens, and the row can be written generically

Medium; likely (lead).

- **Where:** `crates/moqui-mail/src/oauth.rs:153-186`, `:429-432`, `:758-768`; `crates/moqui-entity/src/entity/sensitive.rs:64`. Report: round 3 `A3.md`.
- **Reached by:** a user who may write `moqui.basic.email.EmailOAuthProvider` generically: an `ADMIN` through Tools › Data, entity REST, or a data load. The Admin connection screen shows `tokenUrl` as display only (`ConfigurationConnections.js:1086` `{name: "tokenUrl", label: "Token endpoint", kind: "display"}`), which is the rule the generic tools pass by.
- **What happens:** The provider's client secret may come from a conf property (`<prefix>_client_secret`), which a script cannot read; the token endpoint comes from the row. At the next token refresh of any mailbox of that provider the server posts `client_id`, `client_secret` and that mailbox's refresh token to the row's `tokenUrl`, with no `https` test, on a client of its own that has no address guard (not the link-local one of the shared client either). The same reasoning put `DatabaseConnection` on the restricted list (`sensitive.rs:68-69`: "which a generic write could point at another host to have the password sent there").
- **Evidence:** `oauth.rs:166` `let mut client_secret = pick(property("client_secret"), "clientSecret");` and `:185` `token_url: fill("tokenUrl"),`
- **Evidence:** `oauth.rs:758-763` `("grant_type".to_string(), "refresh_token".to_string()), ("refresh_token".to_string(), refresh.clone()), ("client_id".to_string(), provider.client_id.clone()), ("client_secret".to_string(), provider.client_secret.clone()),` then `:768` `let answer = match post_form(&provider.token_url, &pairs) {`
- **Evidence:** `oauth.rs:431` `CLIENT.get_or_init(|| moqui_lib::http::RestClient::new().timeout(HTTP_TIMEOUT))`
- **Evidence:** `sensitive.rs:64` `const SECRET_CONFIG_ENTITY_KEYS: &[&str] = &["emailserver", "systemmessageremote"];` (`emailoauthprovider` is in no list)
- **Not confirmed:** not run. The generic write path, as for SEC-OUT-81; whether `store#Connection` or another service checks the URL when it is the writer (the screen makes it read only, taken from the one line quoted); `revokeUrl` and `userInfoUrl`, which the reviewer says are used the same way, were not followed. Looked for and not found: a scheme or host test on `tokenUrl` in `Provider::from_row` and `configured` (`oauth.rs:153-232`); the entity in the restricted lists; an address guard on the client.
- **Suggested fix:** Add `emailoauthprovider` to the restricted entities, require `https` for `tokenUrl`, `revokeUrl` and `userInfoUrl` in `Provider::from_row`, and give the client `deny_link_local_addresses(true)` at least.
- **Re-check:** Read `oauth.rs:150-188` (`token_url` straight from the row) and `sensitive.rs` (`emailoauthprovider` is in no list; `emailserver` is). Not read by the lead: `oauth.rs:431`, `:758-768`.

## Development tools

#### SEC-DEV-01. export#Records returns any entity's records to every Dev AIDE tier, including the stored tokens get#Credentials keeps for ADMIN

Medium; reported (likely); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-16: dev-aide `export#Records` (`entities.rs` `export_refused`) refuses a restricted entity for everyone, and a `moqui.security` entity, `DevAideCredential`, `DevAideServer`, `DevAideTaskSource` or any entity with an encrypted field below the full tier with `ADMIN`; its writer is never full, so the text holds no password or encrypted field. `try#Snippet` at the extend tier is code execution by design. Test: `dev-aide/test/EntityModeler.spec.js`.
- **Where:** runtime/components/dev/dev-aide/src/entities.rs:98-131; runtime/components/dev/dev-aide/data/DevAideSecurityData.xml:29-39
- **Reached by:** a member of DEV_AIDE_EXTENDERS or DEV_AIDE_USERS (not ADMIN) on a dev instance, GET /apps/dev/Workspace/exportRecords with an entity name (read-only transition, URL parameters).
- **What happens:** The service needs only the view tier and finds on whatever entity the request names, with no list of refused entities. The DEV_AIDE_APP group is an inheritable ALWAYS/ALL grant for all three groups, so the find below it is permitted for any entity: UserAccount, UserLoginKey, moqui.dev.DevAideCredential, DevAideServer. get#Credentials and store#Credential deliberately require ADMIN and never return the token; this path goes round that.
- **Evidence:** `let context = Context::of(ec, Tier::View)?; let name = entity_name_in(parameters); ... let mut find = context.facade.find(&name)?;` and `<authz artifactAuthzId="DEV_AIDE_APP_EXTENDERS" userGroupId="DEV_AIDE_EXTENDERS" authzTypeEnumId="AUTHZT_ALWAYS" authzActionEnumId="AUTHZA_ALL"/>`
- **Not confirmed:** whether EntityDataWriter emits `encrypt="true"` fields decrypted; whether MAX_EXPORT or the writer excludes any entity. The extend tier can also read the same rows with try#Snippet, so the fix has to cover both.
- **Suggested fix:** Refuse `moqui.security.*`, `moqui.dev.DevAideCredential`, `DevAideServer` and `DevAideTaskSource` (or any entity with an encrypted field) in export#Records below the full tier with ADMIN, and omit encrypted fields from the text.

#### SEC-DEV-05. MailCapture.enable() has no test-run gate, unlike HttpFixtures

Medium; reported (to check); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-16: `MailCapture` (`service/mail_capture.rs`) captures only when `mail_capture::allowed()`, a process flag the `moqui test` runner sets and no server sets: `enable()` elsewhere returns an error, `is_enabled()` is false, and the `mail_capture_sends` property is ignored with a warning. Test: `mail_capture.rs` `the_capture_follows_its_switch_and_keeps_the_latest`.
- **Where:** crates/moqui-core/src/service/mail_capture.rs:176-232; runtime/components/base/framework/conf/MoquiDefaultConf.xml:619
- **Reached by:** any script that can call `ec.factory.getTool('MailCapture')` on any instance purpose, or an operator who sets `mail_capture_sends=true`.
- **What happens:** The tool factory is declared in the default conf and `enable()` sets capture for the whole factory with no check of a test run or of the instance purpose. From then on every send is recorded as sent but kept in memory, and `getMessages()` returns the bodies: password reset links and sign-in codes of any user go to the script instead of the mailbox. HttpFixtures has the gate (`allowed()`, set only by `moqui test`); this tool does not.
- **Evidence:** `"enable" => { capture.set_enabled(Some(true)); Ok(Value::Null) }` and `fn get_instance(&self, _parameters: &[Value]) -> Result<Arc<dyn Tool>, Error> { Ok(Arc::new(MailCaptureTool)) }`
- **Not confirmed:** which script surfaces reach `ec.factory.getTool` for a user who is not already a code author (Dev AIDE try#Snippet on dev does; report, CalcGrid and agent tool scripts were not traced).
- **Suggested fix:** Gate `enable()` and the `mail_capture_sends` property on the same process flag the test runner sets for HttpFixtures.

#### SEC-DEV-06. The demo lock-down leaves many ADMIN grants and permissions in place that were added after it

Medium; reported (to check); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-16: `DemoSecurityData.xml` ends ADMIN's `ADMIN_ACCESS_MANAGE`, `ADMIN_IDENTITY_MANAGE`, `ADMIN_IDENTITY_GLOBAL`, the backup, connection, transfer, settings, operation and constitution permissions, `AgentAdmin`, `AgentSkillAuthor`, `ChatBridgeAdmin`, `VoiceAdmin`, `GIT_CI_ADMIN`, `MONITOR_EVAL_RUN`, `MONITOR_REPLAY`, `MONITOR_PRIVATE_NETWORK` and `EMAIL_ACCOUNT_HOSTED`, and makes `TOOLS_DATABASE_LOAD_ADMIN` and `AIDE_PROJECTS_ADMIN` view-only; `Demo.spec.js` fails on any ADMIN grant or permission of the base seed that the demo neither ends nor allows. The app components' ADMIN grants are not checked by it. Test: `demo/test/Demo.spec.js`. Follow-up (2026-11-16, the owner's decision, commit 8de766db0): it also ends ADMIN's three owner changes, `DRIVE_OWNER_CHANGE`, `EMAIL_ACCOUNT_OWNER_CHANGE` and `REPORT_OWNER_CHANGE` (every visitor is john.doe, so a hand-over moved another visitor's things), and `Demo.spec.js` lists them as ended.
- **Where:** runtime/components/dev/demo/data/DemoSecurityData.xml:21-59
- **Reached by:** anyone signed in as the shared `john.doe` ADMIN on a public demo server.
- **What happens:** The demo data makes ADMIN view-only on nine artifact groups and ends ten permissions. The base seed also gives ADMIN ALL on DASHBOARDS_APP, CALC_GRID, CALC_GRID_AUTHOR, BASIC_COMMS, four REPORT_* groups, TOOLS_DATABASE_LOAD (`run#LoadWrap`) and AIDE_PROJECTS, and the permissions ADMIN_BACKUP_RUN, ADMIN_BACKUP_DOWNLOAD, ADMIN_CONNECTION_MANAGE, ADMIN_TRANSFER_RUN, ADMIN_SETTINGS_MANAGE, ADMIN_IDENTITY_MANAGE, ADMIN_ACCESS_MANAGE, AgentAdmin, AgentSkillAuthor, GIT_CI_ADMIN, ChatBridgeAdmin, VoiceAdmin, MONITOR_EVAL_RUN, MONITOR_REPLAY, REST_SCHEMA, which the demo data does not touch. Any screen outside Tools and System that reaches the services behind them is open to every visitor.
- **Evidence:** demo data ends only `SearchRemote, DashboardRemote, ADMIN_PASSWORD, SSH_SFTP, SSH_GIT, MONITOR_INGEST, REPORT_SAVE_LOCATION, MONITOR_RECOVER, MONITOR_RECOVER_ADMIN, MONITOR_SETTINGS`; base seed has `<moqui.security.UserGroupPermission userGroupId="ADMIN" userPermissionId="ADMIN_BACKUP_DOWNLOAD" fromDate="0"/>` and `TOOLS_DATABASE_LOAD_ADMIN ... AUTHZT_ALWAYS ... AUTHZA_ALL`.
- **Not confirmed:** the screens that call these services were not traced; the `org.moqui.admin` backup, connection and transfer services are called from `tools/screen/System/` screens, which the view-only SYSTEM_APP row covers for transitions that are not read-only. BasicComms chat bridges and voice screens are under `basic-comms/screen/System/` and BasicComms; CalcGrid authoring and the Assist agent were not read.
- **Suggested fix:** Walk each remaining grant on a demo server and end what a visitor does not need; add a spec in the demo component that fails when the base seed gains an ADMIN grant the demo data does not name.
